# Mechanism atlas: modrand_p113 (original)

The network's weights are split EXACTLY into rank-1 mechanisms (SPD). Each mechanism has a readable trigger (when it is needed), scored on held-out inputs (its confidence), and an effect (what removing it changes).

## Whole-network certificate

- Rebuilt from its mechanisms, each switched by its trigger where that trigger is trusted (confidence >= 0.9 on clear-cut cases) AND passed a decision check on the training half (169 of 169 trusted triggers); every other mechanism stays on; switched off only where the trigger gives it under a 5% chance of being needed: keeps **81.9%** of the network's decisions; the task rule alone keeps 0.8%.
- The mechanisms alone (all on, the small leftover residual removed): 5.1%. Every trigger used (no confidence bar): 4.3%. Mechanisms switched by their own learned importances: 68.2%. Other gates, for the record: v1 72.4%, v2 81.9%, v3 (every trusted trigger, no decision check) 81.9%.

## Findings

1. The weights split exactly into 487 mechanisms; 169 have a trusted trigger (held-out confidence >= 0.9 on clear-cut cases), 5 are always needed.
1b. 169 of the 482 mechanisms that are not always on have a task-level explanation (a trigger in task variables only, confidence >= 0.9 on clear-cut cases).
2. 169 of the 169 trusted triggers use a task variable (not only neuron states). Most used: token here (49), position (49), 44·token here mod 113 (22), 27·token here mod 113 (20), 19·token here mod 113 (18).
4. Rebuilt from mechanisms switched by trusted triggers: 81.9% of decisions (task rule 0.8%).
6. Structure (a 'rule' carries over to token values never seen when fitting it; a 'lookup' only describes the cases seen). Trusted mechanisms by matrix: W_E: 1 rule, 63 lookup; H0_W_K: 2 lookup; H0_W_V: 1 rule, 16 lookup; H0_W_O: 1 lookup; H1_W_V: 5 rule, 28 lookup; H1_W_O: 7 lookup; H2_W_K: 1 lookup; H2_W_V: 19 lookup; H2_W_O: 7 lookup; H3_W_K: 1 lookup; H3_W_V: 15 lookup; H3_W_O: 1 lookup; W_in: 1 lookup. Frequencies most used by rule-labelled mechanisms (basis, count): 37 (line, 3), 8 (line, 2), 25 (line, 2), 28 (line, 2), 19 (line, 1), 56 (line, 1), 50 (line, 1), 11 (line, 1).
